Thus, the main result is conditional rather than universal: label-derived relational evidence is useful only where held-out validation supports it. The framework couples competitive ranking performance with an explicit label-provenance contract, finite selection policy, failure accounting, and review-budget evaluation, providing an auditable knowledge-based decision pipeline for graph fraud detection.

</details>


### [264] [Heterogeneous Agent Cohorts for Safe Open-Ended Exploration with Runtime Constraint Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11226)
*Tengjiao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种角色分离的LLM多智能体框架，通过Disrupter、Validator、Broker三个专业化角色分工协作，并引入基于MCTS的Scars机制将失败经验编译为可继承的约束补丁，在空间语义沙盒中实现了更高的目标达成率、零安全违规和显著的成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体面临两难困境：严格的安全指令限制了创造力，而赋予自由又导致安全违规频发。作者希望在不牺牲创造力的前提下实现可靠的安全控制。

Method: 提出角色分离框架：(1) Disrupter生成非常规方案；(2) Validator在工具网关执行运行时检查；(3) Broker引入遥远但相关的类比。失败经验通过MCTS编译为紧凑的、带签名的约束补丁（Scars），被缓存并继承给后续智能体队列。同时引入基于信用的通信分配分数（CAS）限制出站带宽。

Result: 在空间语义沙盒中（N=20, p<0.01），该方法在远程目标达成上优于辩论方法，Validator阻止了所有已执行的安全违规，Scars减少了15.1%的令牌消耗（避免冗余验证检查），CAS在资源约束下降低了55.9%的总令牌成本。

Conclusion: 通过角色分离和失败经验的编译复用，可以在保持LLM智能体创造力的同时实现有效的安全约束和成本优化，为多智能体系统的安全高效运行提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLM agents today are caught in an awkward bind. Lock them down with static safety instructions and they rarely venture beyond the obvious; give them free reign with tools and multi-agent debate, and safety violations quickly follow. Rather than forcing a single model to juggle both creativity and caution, we separate the concerns across specialized roles. A Disrupter generates unconventional proposals, a Validator enforces hard runtime checks at the tool gateway, and a Broker pulls in distant but relevant analogies. Failures are not discarded -- they are compiled, via MCTS, into compact, signed constraint patches we call Scars. These patches are cached locally and inherited by future cohorts, turning repeated failures into reusable, low-cost runtime constraints. In a spatial-semantic sandbox (N=20 runs, p<0.01), our cohort reaches remote targets where debate fails, the Validator prevents all executed breaches, and Scars reduce token consumption by 15.1% by avoiding redundant validator checks. Furthermore, credit-based Communication Allocation Scores (CAS) restrict outbound bandwidth, reducing overall token costs by 55.9% under resource constraints.

</details>


### [265] [Bringing Back Rule Induction to Fluid Intelligence Research? An Initial Validation of the ARC-AGI Benchmark in Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11263)
*Jasmin Thelen,Oliver Wilhelm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在人类样本中检验了ARC-AGI基准的心理测量学特性，发现其具有良好的心理测量学属性，与图形流体智力高度相关（ρ = .63），与图形原创性关联较弱，初步支持其作为人类流体智力测量工具的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体智力测量存在两种竞争视角：工作记忆容量观与关系归纳能力观。当前测量实践主要受限于前者（使用有限重复规则），而后者虽常见于定义却罕见于实际测量。ARC-AGI基准主要要求规则归纳能力，被提出作为人类和AI系统的流体智力测量工具，但其心理测量学特性尚未在人类样本中检验。

Method: 对100名参与者进行ARC-AGI项目汇编测试，分析其心理测量学特性，并通过与图形推理测验（测量图形流体智力）和图形原创性的相关分析考察其效标关联效度。

Result: ARC-AGI项目汇编表现出良好的心理测量学属性，与图形流体智力测验的相关系数为ρ = .63，与图形原创性的关联较弱。

Conclusion: 研究初步支持ARC-AGI作为人类流体智力测量工具的有效性。未来研究应纳入更多规则归纳任务及多变量协变量。建议将AI基准系统性地嵌入人类认知能力的效度网络，以促进跨学科合作与更系统的评估。

Abstract: Two competing perspectives on fluid intelligence (gf) measures propose that performance is primarily constrained either by working memory capacity or by the ability to induce novel relations. The first perspective is currently dominant in measurement, as evident from the use of a limited set of recurring rules, whereas the second perspective is reflected in many definitions but rarely present in measurement. The ARC-AGI benchmark predominantly requires rule induction and was proposed as a measure of gf for both humans and artificial systems. However, its psychometric properties have not yet been examined in human samples. We therefore investigated the psychometric characteristics and nomological network of ARC-AGI in a first study with 100 participants. A compilation of ARC-AGI items showed good psychometric properties and correlated substantially with figural fluid intelligence as measured by a figural reasoning test (\r{ho} = .63). Associations with figural originality were weak. These findings provide initial support for the validity of ARC-AGI as a measure of human fluid intelligence. Future research should include more rule induction tasks as well as additional multivariate covariates. This study is unusual by studying a task in humans that was initially designed for machines. We suggest systematically embedding AI benchmarks into the nomological network of human cognitive abilities to enable more systematic evaluation and interdisciplinary cooperation.

</details>


### [266] [Valid $\ne$ Necessary: Diagnosing Latent Inefficiency in Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11266)
*Daeyeop Lee,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CAID指标（基于信息论的无训练度量）来识别CoT推理中的低效步骤，并基于此设计PACE压缩策略，在GSM8K、StrategyQA和ARC-Challenge上实现31-53%的token压缩且保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理步评估器能检测逻辑错误和事实错误，但无法惩罚那些有效但低效的推理步骤（如循环推理、过度分解），导致CoT推理产生大量冗余token消耗。

Method: 1) 构建RIV-GSM8K诊断基准，注入5种不同类型的低效推理；2) 提出CAID（上下文感知信息密度），基于信息论的无训练度量来识别低效用步骤；3) 设计PACE后验压缩策略，利用CAID进行步骤剪枝。

Result: 在GSM8K、StrategyQA和ARC-Challenge上，PACE将token消耗减少31-53%，同时保持准确率；控制实验表明CAID优于随机剪枝和PRM-based压缩基线。

Conclusion: CAID能有效从推理链中蒸馏出信息冗余而不损害推理有效性，为CoT推理的效率优化提供了可行的诊断与压缩方案。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet it often incurs substantial computational costs due to over-reasoning: the generation of redundant, verbose, or irrelevant steps. While existing reasoning step evaluators effectively detect logical fallacies and factual errors, our analysis reveals a critical blind spot: they fail to penalize valid but inefficient reasoning steps that inflate token usage without contributing to the solution. To systematically diagnose this limitation, we introduce RIV-GSM8K, a diagnostic benchmark injected with five distinct types of inefficiencies, including circular reasoning and excessive decomposition. Diagnostic experiments reveal that state-of-the-art evaluators struggle to distinguish these inefficiencies from necessary reasoning. To address this gap, we propose CAID (Context-Aware Information Density), a training-free metric grounded in information theory that identifies low-utility steps. To validate the metric's practical utility, we apply it within PACE, a post-hoc compression strategy. Additional control experiments show that the gains of PACE are not explained by trivial pruning: compared with random step removal and PRM-based compression baselines, it preserves accuracy at substantially higher compression rates. Empirical results on GSM8K, StrategyQA, and ARC-Challenge demonstrate that PACE reduces token consumption by 31-53% while maintaining accuracy, confirming that CAID successfully distills informational froth from reasoning chains without compromising deductive validity.

</details>


### [267] [Efficient Test-Time Optimization for Multi-Agent Proof Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11307)
*Tian-Shuo Liu,Shiyuan Zhang,Zijie Geng,Haoyu Liu,Runjie Xu,Pengyuan Wang,Lei Yuan,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToMap是一个多智能体框架，通过Decomposer-Formalizer-Prover流水线和高效的测试时优化，将证明级自动形式化建模为分解-形式化-验证流程，在ProofFlowBench上相比最优方法提升19.0%，且测试时成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明级自动形式化（proof autoformalization）相比语句级形式化更具挑战性，需要协调多个证明步骤中的主张、上下文和依赖关系，但目前研究不足；现有方法要么依赖昂贵的模型训练，要么在推理时进行过度、无引导的修复。

Method: 提出ToMap多智能体框架，包含三个角色：Decomposer（将自然语言证明分解为原子化的自包含证明单元）、Formalizer（将分解后的单元形式化）、Prover（验证形式化证明）。通过瓶颈分析识别Decomposer为关键瓶颈，聚焦测试时计算资源对Decomposer进行迭代优化；优化循环受GEPA启发，在候选分解上进化prompt，利用形式化验证进度和语义证明评估标准构建Pareto前沿指导下一轮分解更新。

Result: 在ProofFlowBench上，ToMap相比此前最优方法在语法正确性和语义忠实性上均提升19.0%，同时测试时成本更低；规模分析表明大多数增益在少量分解进化迭代内即可获得，可用于指导测试时预算选择。

Conclusion: 通过瓶颈分析将测试时计算聚焦于Decomposer的迭代优化，结合形式化验证和语义评估构建Pareto前沿引导搜索，ToMap在证明级自动形式化任务上实现了更优的性能和更低的成本。

Abstract: Full-proof autoformalization bridges extensive mathematical proofs in natural language with formally validated reasoning, offering a pathway to elevate the ceiling of verifiable mathematical reasoning. Unlike statement-level formalization, proof autoformalization is a long-horizon challenge requiring coordination of claims, contexts, and dependencies across many proof steps, yet has only recently come under focused study. Current approaches either rely on costly model training or apply excessive, unguided repair at inference time. To this end, we introduce ToMap, a multi-agent framework that structures proof autoformalization as a Decomposer-Formalizer-Prover pipeline with efficient test-time optimization guided by formal verification and semantic rubrics for proof quality. Rather than distributing test-time compute across all agents, we perform bottleneck analysis and identify the Decomposer as the critical bottleneck: the quality of its atomic, self-contained proof units directly determines whether downstream agents can successfully formalize and prove each step. ToMap therefore treats the Formalizer and Prover as downstream executors and efficiently focuses test-time compute on Decomposer refinement. This refinement follows a loop inspired by GEPA, evolving prompts over candidate decompositions and using formal verification progress together with semantic proof rubrics to define a Pareto frontier that guides the next decomposition update. Experiments on ProofFlowBench show that ToMap improves over the best previous method by 19.0% when evaluated by both syntactic correctness and semantic faithfulness, while requiring lower test-time cost. Scaling analysis shows that most gains emerge within a few iterations of decomposition evolution, guiding test-time budget selection.

</details>


### [268] [Calibrated e-CUSUM Decoding for Quantized Reasoning Models: Why Token Log-Probability Is the Wrong Observable for Decoding Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11317)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出中心化词元对数概率增量（log p(w_t)+H_t）作为解码器监控信号存在根本缺陷——它在模型自身采样律下是零均值鞅，无法检测退化；提出一种无需训练的控制器，结合退化感知告警分数与类e过程顺序检测器，在GSM8K/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B上实现INT4精度从63%提升至69%（p=0.18），代价为28%词元预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化部署小规模推理模型时，链式推理可能退化，亟需有效的解码器端监控与干预机制，但现有候选信号（中心化词元对数概率增量）在理论上无法胜任此任务。

Method: 提出无需训练的控制器，包含：(i) 融合词元不确定性与显式逐字重复检测的退化感知告警分数；(ii) 受e过程启发的校准顺序检测器。原始乘积过程在条件零均值原假设下满足Ville有效性，实际部署采用CUSUM下限统计量作为经验性变化检测器。

Result: 在GSM8K上，INT4精度从63%提升至69%（配对McNemar检验p=0.18，n=100），监控器从93-95%触发率变为选择性检测失败轨迹（φ≈0.3，精确率≈0.6，基率0.38），词元预算成本28%。发现非终止而非循环是GSM8K上的主要失败模式。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是方法论的：解释了中心化词元对数概率为何不适用于解码器监控，并提供了一个校准的、经谨慎评估的替代方案。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization makes small reasoning models inexpensive to deploy but can degrade their chains of thought. This motivates decoder-side monitors that intervene when generation becomes unreliable. We show that a natural candidate, the centered token log-probability increment $\log p(w_t)+H_t$, is the wrong observable for this purpose. Under the model's own sampling law it is a mean-zero martingale by construction, so it measures sampling self-consistency rather than trajectory health and is nearly silent during confident repetition, where both $\log p(w_t)$ and entropy are close to zero. We introduce a training-free decoding controller that combines (i) a degeneration-aware alarm score fusing token uncertainty with explicit verbatim repetition and (ii) a calibrated e-process-inspired sequential detector. The raw product process is Ville-valid under a conditional-mean null, while the deployed CUSUM-floored statistic is treated as an empirical change detector because the score is history-dependent and autocorrelated. On GSM8K with DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B in FP16 and INT4, calibration turns a monitor that fires on 93--95% of generations into a selective detector of failing traces ($φ\approx 0.3$, precision $\approx 0.6$ against a 0.38 base rate). In this pilot, the controller reduces measured verbatim-degeneration signals and yields a positive but statistically inconclusive INT4 accuracy change from 63% to 69% (paired McNemar $p=0.18$, $n=100$), at a 28% token-budget cost. We also find that non-termination, rather than looping, is the dominant failure mode on GSM8K. The main contribution is methodological: an explanation of why centered token log-probability is inadequate for decoder monitoring and a calibrated, cautiously evaluated replacement.

</details>


### [269] [Verifier-Guided Twelve-Tone Composition: A Generate-Verify-Repair Harness for Symbolic Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11334)
*Congren Dai,Danni Zhao,Enyang Liu,Michael Ching Yam,Zhancheng Guo,Siyi Gu,Wentao Yang,Bo Dai,Xiaobing Li,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号混合框架，通过“生成-验证-修复-追踪”循环包装语言模型，用于生成十二音序列。该框架显著提升了局部一致性，在40个控制任务中，审计交付率从13.3%提升至48.1%，碰撞与序列一致性检查通过率从33.5%提升至58.3%，退化率保持在0.05以下。五位专家盲评也显示对框架输出有显著偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的十二音乐谱表面合法但容易退化为退化纹理（即不合法的序列结构），需要一种方法来提升生成结果的局部一致性和整体合法性。

Method: 提出一种神经符号混合框架，将语言模型作为提议器包裹在“生成-验证-修复-追踪”（generate-verify-repair-trace）循环中，结合符号验证器进行迭代优化。

Result: 在40个控制任务和四个配对模型上，审计交付率从13.3%提升至48.1%；碰撞与序列一致性检查通过率从33.5%提升至58.3%；退化率保持在0.05附近（包括对抗性提示下）；五位专家盲评显示框架输出在一致性、感知合法性、连贯性和整体质量上均优于原始生成。

Conclusion: 神经符号混合框架能有效提升十二音序列生成的局部一致性和合法性，同时保持低退化率，并得到专家评估的认可。

Abstract: Large language models can produce superficially legal twelve-tone scores that collapse into degenerate textures. We introduce a neuro-symbolic harness that wraps a language-model proposer in a generate-verify-repair-trace loop with symbolic verification. The complete pipeline improves event-local consistency without claiming whole-piece legality. Across 40 controlled tasks and four paired models, audited delivery yield rises from 13.3% under raw generation to 48.1% with the harness, which explicitly abstains otherwise. The pass rate of a narrower collision and serialisation-consistency check rises from 33.5% to 58.3%, while degeneracy remains near 0.05, including under exploratory adversarial prompting. A blinded evaluation by five experts also shows a descriptive aggregate preference for harness candidates over raw generation in adherence, perceived legality, coherence, and overall quality.

</details>


### [270] [AutoVSR: Automatic Visual-to-Symbolic Reasoning for Symbolic Expression Generation from Circuit Schematic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11338)
*Zhe Xiao,Longfei Li,Xu He,Haoying Wu,Zixing Zhang,Mingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoVSR是一个自动化框架，通过将电路图重建为可执行中间表示并结合符号求解器，实现从电路图像到符号表达式的准确生成，相比现有方法准确率提升30%~59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从电路原理图直接推导符号表达式需要准确的视觉到符号的结构构建和正确的多步符号推导，这两个环节都对正确性有严格要求，现有方法难以同时满足。

Method: 提出AutoVSR框架，包含两个关键创新：(1) 基于组件规则检索和验证反馈的中间表示(IR)构建方法；(2) 基于规划智能体和符号工具库的符号求解器，用于可靠的多步推导。

Result: 在符号表达式生成任务上，相比端到端VLM方法准确率提升30.01%~59.45%，相比专用方法提升41.96%~51.84%；同时推理成本和计算效率优于闭源SOTA VLM。

Conclusion: AutoVSR通过可执行中间表示与符号求解器的结合，有效解决了视觉到符号表达式的生成难题，在准确率和效率上均取得显著优势。

Abstract: Symbolic expressions can effectively characterize and predict circuit behavior, but deriving them directly from circuit schematics is challenging. This process requires accurate visual-to-symbolic construction of circuit structure from images and correct multi-step symbolic derivation, both of which impose strict correctness requirements. This work proposes AutoVSR, an automated framework for visual-to-symbolic generation of circuit expressions using Vision Language Models (VLMs). By reconstructing circuit diagrams into an executable intermediate representation (Executable IR) and leveraging a symbolic solver for reasoning, AutoVSR significantly improves the accuracy of symbolic expression generation. AutoVSR introduces two key innovations: an IR construction method guided by component rule retrieval and verification-based feedback, and a symbolic solver implemented as a planning agent equipped with a symbolic tool library for reliable multi-step derivation. Compared with end-to-end VLM approaches and specialized methods on the main symbolic expression generation task, AutoVSR achieves accuracy improvements of 30.01--59.45% and 41.96--51.84%, respectively. Moreover, AutoVSR surpasses closed-source state-of-the-art VLMs in inference cost and computational efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/LongfeiLi1/AutoVSR.

</details>


### [271] [Compile, Then Page: Executable SOP Programs and a Capability-Gated Runtime for Procedural LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11346)
*Chenglin Yu,Li Yin,Ying Yu,Hongxia Yang,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种程序引导（PG）的堆栈机框架，将SOP约束编译为可执行伪代码，由LLM进行语义执行。通过SOPBench三臂实验，证明编译表示优于原始散文（最高提升16.0分），而运行时引导具有能力门控特性：强模型获益（正域对比率58:19和75:31），弱模型受损。全程序指针消融实验恢复了大部分拒绝增益，选择性可见性提升较小。探针和审计测量将差距归因于自发性状态纪律而非重建能力。Bank任务三臂得分从70.4升至86.4再到92.8，拒绝正确率100%。实践建议：优先编译；仅在模型通过纪律检查后启用活动帧分页。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业代理需要遵循长程、条件性、安全关键的标准操作程序（SOP），但现有LLM在直接遵循自然语言描述的SOP时表现不佳，需要更可靠的表示和执行机制。

Method: 将机器可读的SOP约束编译为可执行伪代码，使用程序引导（PG）的堆栈机运行，其中活动帧由LLM进行语义执行。设计了SOPBench三臂实验（官方散文、编译文本、编译文本+运行时引导），在6个模型上评估。进行全程序指针消融和选择性可见性实验，以及探针和审计测量分析。

Result: 编译文本从未显著损害性能，最高提升16.0分。强模型（58:19和75:31不一致对）在PG下正向域对比显著，弱模型受损。全程序指针消融恢复大部分增益，选择性可见性提升较小。Bank任务三臂得分：70.4→86.4→92.8，拒绝正确率100%。

Conclusion: 编译优先于原始散文表示；运行时活动帧分页应仅在模型通过状态纪律检查后启用，因为其有效性依赖于模型内在的纪律能力。

Abstract: Enterprise agents must follow long-horizon, conditional, safety-critical standard operating procedures (SOPs). We compile machine-readable SOP constraints into executable pseudo-code and run them with a program-guided (PG) stack machine that pages the active frame while an LLM performs semantic execution. A three-arm SOPBench study across six models separates representation from runtime: compiled text never significantly hurts and gains up to 16.0 points where official prose underperforms. Runtime guidance is capability-gated. Two strong models independently show positive seven-domain PG contrasts (58:19 and 75:31 discordant pairs), whereas weak models are harmed. A full-program cursor ablation (active frame first, complete program retained) recovers much of the strong-model refusal gain; selective visibility adds a smaller improvement. Paired probe and audit measurements track this divide to spontaneous state discipline rather than reconstruction ability. On Bank the three primary arms rise from 70.4 to 86.4 to 92.8, with 100% refusal correctness. Practical guidance: compile first; enable active-frame paging only after a model-level discipline check.

</details>


### [272] [From Neural Network Decisions to Training Cases: An Exact Account via Case-Based Decision Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11347)
*Manli Yan,Yuebin Lin,Yaowen Yu,Yong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于OLS探针的案例级审计方法，能够在固定神经网络表示上将动作分数精确分解为训练案例收益的加权和，系数由经验Gram几何决定。该方法无需重新训练或访问原始优化轨迹，在多个任务上实现了最高的Top-30一致性，并能有效重建案例级偏好结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险决策领域（如医疗诊断、信用审批、能源竞价）需要案例级审计证据，以追溯哪些训练案例支持了某个决策及其结果。现有方法缺乏这种精细化的可解释性。

Method: 在固定的神经网络表示上拟合一个OLS（普通最小二乘法）顶层线性探针，将每个动作分数分解为训练案例收益的加权和，权重由经验Gram矩阵的几何结构决定。该方法无需重新训练模型或访问原始优化轨迹。

Result: 在合成CBDT、PJM能源竞价、Adult收入和Default Credit等多个任务上，该方法在Top-30一致性指标上优于对比的归因基线方法，同时在支持重建任务上保持竞争力。探针保真度通过分数重建进行衡量。

Conclusion: OLS探针提供了一种高效且可解释的案例级审计工具，能够追踪分数来源、衡量动作一致性并识别弱支持，为高风险场景下的神经网络决策提供了可行的审计信号。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly guide decisions in high-stakes domains such as medical diagnosis, credit approval, and energy bidding. Audit in these settings requires case-level evidence: which training cases support an action and what outcomes they carried. Case-based decision theory (CBDT) formalizes this reasoning by aggregating outcome support from remembered cases. We show that an OLS action readout fitted on a fixed neural representation admits an exact case-based decomposition. Each action score is a weighted sum of training-case returns, with coefficients determined by empirical Gram geometry. We identify a sufficient regime for CBDT similarity semantics; outside it, the coefficients should generally be treated as signed Gram-geometric influence. The decomposition yields audit signals that trace scores to training cases, measure action coherence, and identify weak support. Across synthetic CBDT, PJM, Adult Income, and Default Credit tasks, the method recovers case-level preference structure and achieves the highest mean Top-30 consistency among compared attribution baselines, while remaining competitive on support reconstruction. The audit requires only fitting an OLS top-layer probe, without retraining the representation or accessing the original optimization trajectory; probe fidelity is measured by score reconstruction.

</details>


### [273] [StructAgent: Harness Long-horizon Digital Agents with Unified Causal Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11388)
*Wenyi Wu,Sibo Zhu,Kun Zhou,Aayush Salvi,Zixuan Song,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StructAgent框架，通过统一的因果状态表示和结构化工作流来显式管理任务进度，显著提升了LLM/VLM在长时程计算机使用任务中的表现，在OSWorld-Verified上将Qwen3.5-27B从31.6%提升至62.2%，并实现78.9%的新开源SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字代理在长时程任务中仅依赖原始交互历史，导致任务进度难以解释、验证和恢复，限制了可靠的长时程执行能力。

Method: 提出StructAgent框架，包含：（1）统一状态模块，维护紧凑可验证的任务进度因果表示；（2）结构化工作流，通过验证器支撑的状态转换来规范进度更新，支持显式进度检查点、证据驱动的任务完成、定向失败恢复和工具支撑的执行。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified上，Qwen3.5-9B从27.0%提升至46.9%，Qwen3.5-27B从31.6%提升至62.2%，MiniMax-M3达到78.9%的新开源SOTA。该框架也能泛化到Minecraft环境。

Conclusion: StructAgent通过结构化状态与工作流设计，有效提升了多种LLM/VLM在长时程任务中的可靠性和性能，并具有良好的跨环境泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) have enabled increasingly capable digital agents for computer use. However, real-world tasks are often long-horizon and involve evolving contexts containing accumulated observations, intermediate edits, failed attempts, and partially completed executions. Existing agents typically operate over raw interaction history, making task progress difficult to interpret, verify, and recover, which ultimately limits reliable long-horizon execution. In this paper, we argue that addressing this challenge requires explicitly structuring both the agent's state and workflow around a unified causal representation of task progress. We present \textbf{StructAgent}, a state-centered framework that introduces a unified state for maintaining compact, verifiable task progress and a structured workflow that regulates progress through verifier-backed state transitions. Building on this design, StructAgent further enables explicit progress checkpointing, evidence-driven task completion, targeted failure recovery, and tool-supported execution, while ensuring that all progress updates remain grounded in verification. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StructAgent consistently improves a wide range of LLM and VLM backbones on long-horizon computer-use tasks. On OSWorld-Verified, it improves Qwen3.5-9B from 27.0\% to 46.9\% success rate and Qwen3.5-27B from 31.6\% to 62.2\%, while achieving a new open-source state of the art of 78.9\% with MiniMax-M3. Moreover, the same framework generalizes beyond desktop environments to Minecraft, demonstrating the generality of our design.

</details>


### [274] [Omni-Decision: A Progressive Evidence-State Agent System for Omni-Modal QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11433)
*Ming Ma,Yi Zhu,Yiran Zhong,Feida Zhu,Weigao Sun,Junhan Shi,Lingrui Mei,Tianming Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Omni-Decision 是一种无需训练的证据状态系统，通过维护结构化的证据状态（已确认证据、未解决冲突、事实与计算依赖、开放证据需求），将全模态QA转化为查询范围的证据闭合过程，在OmniGAIA和WorldSense上分别达到45.6%和58.3%的准确率，比基线提升27.3和30.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态智能体系统将证据分散在草稿板、工具轨迹或自由形式的历史记录中，难以追踪已证实的证据、缺失的证据以及何时证据足够回答问题。

Method: 提出Omni-Decision，一种无需训练的证据状态系统。为每个查询维护结构化证据状态（含已确认证据、未解决冲突、事实与计算依赖、开放证据需求），通过共享状态视图条件化规划、证据获取、验证、修复和最终化。来自媒体、网页、计算和验证模块的异构观测经规范化、判断和确定性状态更新提交。

Result: OmniGAIA数据集上准确率45.6%，WorldSense上58.3%，分别比基线提升27.3和30.2个百分点。无状态消融实验和轨迹审计进一步验证了显式证据状态控制在多步全模态证据搜寻中的作用。

Conclusion: 显式证据状态控制能够有效支持多步全模态证据搜寻任务，实现有针对性的证据获取、保留稀疏跨模态线索，并提供可检查的修复和停止控制。

Abstract: Omni-modal evidence-seeking QA requires agents to answer questions whose evidence is sparsely distributed across videos, audio, images, web pages, and computation results. Existing agentic multimodal systems often leave evidence in scratchpads, tool trajectories, or free-form histories, making it difficult to track what has been grounded, what remains missing, and when the evidence is sufficient to answer. We propose Omni-Decision, a training-free evidence-state system that turns omni-modal QA into a query-scoped evidence-closure process. For each query, Omni-Decision maintains a structured evidence state containing confirmed evidence, unresolved conflicts, fact and computation dependencies, and open evidence needs. A shared state view conditions planning, evidence acquisition, validation, repair, and finalization. Heterogeneous observations from media, web, computation, and verification modules are normalized, judged, and committed through deterministic state updates. This design enables targeted evidence acquisition, preserves sparse cross-modal cues, and provides inspectable control over repair and stopping. Omni-Decision achieves 45.6% accuracy on OmniGAIA and 58.3% on WorldSense, improving over the baselines by +27.3 and +30.2 percentage points, respectively. No-state ablations and trajectory audits further support the role of explicit evidence-state control in multi-step omni-modal evidence seeking.

</details>


### [275] [The Ebb and Flow of Multimodal Focus: Scheduling Visual Relay Windows for Grounded VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11436)
*Wencheng Ye,Yi Bin,Yujuan Ding,Hongye Fang,Zheng Wang,Xing Xu,Jingkuan Song,Yun Zhang,Sirui Da,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现VLM在多模态推理中存在视觉证据不稳定的问题，揭示了三阶段注意力再分配机制（早期问题条件化、中期视觉主导中继、后期答案形成），提出视觉中继窗口（VRW）概念，并基于此设计了任务自适应的推理时控制框架TRACE，通过轻量模块在预填充和解码阶段重塑中继分配，在多个VLM骨干和基准上平均提升4.33分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在多模态推理基准上表现越来越好，但视觉证据一旦进入语言栈就会变得不稳定，削弱了基于证据的推理能力。作者旨在理解这种脆弱的内部机制并寻找改进方法。

Method: 1) 通过机械论视角分析VLM内部动态，发现稳定的三阶段多模态注意力再分配模式；2) 定义关键中间阶段为视觉中继窗口（VRW），并分析其几何特性；3) 提出TRACE框架，包含轻量级训练模块，在预填充阶段重塑中继分配，在解码阶段保持视觉支持。

Result: 在4个开源VLM骨干和7个基准上，TRACE在对接地敏感的设置上平均提升4.33分，最高提升6.6分，同时在推理密集型任务上也有提升。

Conclusion: 显式控制多模态注意力在深度维度上的分布，为增强基于证据的多模态推理提供了一种统一且有效的机制。

Abstract: Vision-language models increasingly succeed on multimodal reasoning benchmarks, yet their visual evidence often becomes unstable once it enters the language stack, weakening evidence-grounded reasoning. To understand this fragility, we examine the internal dynamics of VLMs through a mechanistic lens and uncover a stable three-stage redistribution of multimodal attention focus across depth: an early question-conditioned organization, a critical middle visual-dominant relay, and a late return to answer formation. We operationalize the middle phase as the Visual Relay Window (VRW), and show that its geometry varies with task demand, is causally tied to grounded generation, and distinguishes unsupported answers from stronger reasoning trajectories. Guided by this internal rhythm, we propose TRACE, a task-adaptive inference-time control framework with lightweight trained modules. It reshapes relay allocation during prefill and preserves assembled visual support after handoff during decoding. Across four open-weight VLM backbones and seven benchmarks, TRACE delivers large gains on grounding-sensitive settings, improving them by 4.33 points on average and by up to 6.6 points, while also improving reasoning-heavy tasks. These results show that explicitly controlling multimodal focus across depth offers a unified and effective mechanism for strengthening evidence-grounded multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [276] [Enhancing Query Efficiency for d-DNNF Representations Through Preprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11492)
*Jean Marie Lagniez,Emmanuel Lonca*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究面向CNF公式模型访问的预处理技术，发现多数不保等价性的预处理器不适用于均匀采样、直接模型访问和模型枚举任务，而保模型计数的预处理器结合相关信息维护可有效提升性能。实验表明，该方法在将CNF编译为d-DNNF表示时能显著提高模型访问查询效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预处理技术多面向 SAT 求解优化，但面向模型访问任务（均匀采样、直接模型访问、模型枚举）时，许多预处理器因不保持公式等价性而无法适用，亟需探索适合这类任务的预处理方案。

Method: 系统分析现有预处理技术对三类模型访问任务的适用性，提出利用保模型计数的预处理器，并保留相关预处理信息以实现兼容与加速；通过在多个领域基准测试集上进行实验验证。

Result: 保模型计数的预处理器可有效用于模型访问任务；在CNF公式编译为d-DNNF表示后，所提预处理方法在模型访问查询上带来显著性能提升，且具备高效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 保模型计数的预处理技术是提升模型访问效率的有效途径，尤其在与d-DNNF编译结合时效果显著；传统不保等价性的预处理器不适合此类任务。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate preprocessing techniques aimed at improving the efficiency of accessing models of propositional formulas represented in conjunctive normal form (CNF). We focus on three fundamental tasks: uniform sampling, direct model access, and model enumeration. Our analysis reveals that most state-of-the-art preprocessors, when they do not preserve formula equivalence, are generally unsuitable for these tasks. In contrast, we demonstrate that preprocessors which preserve model counts can be effectively leveraged, provided relevant preprocessing information is maintained. To validate our approach, we perform extensive experiments on a diverse suite of benchmarks from multiple domains. The experimental results show that our preprocessing methods are both efficient and robust, yielding significant performance improvements for model access queries when CNF formulas are compiled into d-DNNF representations.

</details>


### [277] [Comparative Analysis of GAT and BERT for Human-Like Playtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11501)
*Kleio Fragkedaki,Theodoros Panagiotakopoulos,Matteo Biasielli,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了两种通用网络架构（Transformer/BERT 和 图注意力模型 GAT）来建模玩家在《糖果粉碎传奇》中的行为，相比传统 CNN 方法在复杂棋盘配置上表现更优，且减少了对特征工程的持续维护需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的玩家行为建模方法难以捕捉完整的玩家策略多样性，且需要大量特征工程和网络架构调整，尤其在引入新游戏机制时模型需要持续修改。因此需要一种更通用的表示方法来减少或消除特征工程的维护负担。

Method: 提出了两种通用网络架构：(a)基于Transformer的BERT模型和(b)图注意力模型GAT，用于有效捕捉《糖果粉碎传奇》游戏棋盘的 relational structure（关系结构），并与CNN基线方法进行对比实验。

Result: 在具有挑战性的棋盘配置上，所提出的BERT和GAT模型相比CNN基线方法取得了更好的性能表现，验证了通用表示的优越性。

Conclusion: 通用化网络架构（Transformer和GAT）能够更有效地捕捉游戏棋盘的关系结构，在预测玩家行为方面优于传统CNN方法，且降低了对持续特征工程维护的需求，为设计更智能的预测性游戏测试模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Accurately modeling and understanding player experience is crucial for designing engaging puzzle games. To achieve this, a common approach involves collecting diverse user data to train predictive playtesting models that mimic player behavior. However, existing data-driven methods often lack the ability to capture the full range of player strategies and require extensive feature engineering and network architecture modeling. This limitation becomes particularly evident when new game mechanics or features are introduced, which necessitate continual adjustments to the models. To addrss these challenges, we propose a more generalized representation that reduces - or even eliminates - the need for ongoing feature-engineering maintenance. Specifically, we investigate two general-purpose network architectures: (a) a transformer-based model (BERT) and (b) a graph attention model (GAT), both of which are designed to effectively capture the relational structure of Candy Crush Saga (CCS) game boards. Our experiments compare these approaches to Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) baselines, revealing better performance on challenging board configurations and underscoring the benefits of our generalizable representation.

</details>


### [278] [Learning Residual Kinematic Corrections for Continuous Neural Decoding via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11530)
*Jiamian Li,Niall McShane,Attila Korik,Naomi du Bois,Karl McCreadie,Leen Jabban,Benjamin Metcalfe,Özgür Şimşek,Damien Coyle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CNN-LSTM-RL两阶段解码框架，通过强化学习对CNN-LSTM解码器输出的运动轨迹进行残差校正，在2D和VR环境下分别提升相关系数41.5%和21.2%，降低RMSE 40.2%和38.2%，无需额外神经数据即可增强3D运动想象BCI解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于非侵入式EEG的3D运动想象BCI存在信号变异性大和解码残差误差的问题，现有CNN-LSTM等深度学习架构虽能捕捉时空动态，但预测轨迹中仍存在系统性残差误差，需要一种可扩展的残差校正方法。

Method: 提出两阶段解码框架：(1) 第一阶段使用CNN-LSTM进行初始连续运动想象解码；(2) 第二阶段使用离线训练的强化学习（RL）代理对CNN-LSTM输出的预测运动轨迹进行残差校正，RL代理无需直接输入EEG信号，仅基于预测轨迹优化与目标轨迹的匹配精度。

Result: 在2D环境下，平均相关系数从0.5076提升至0.7181（+41.5%，p=0.0005），RMSE从0.0890降至0.0532（-40.2%，p<0.0001）；在VR环境下，相关系数从0.6420提升至0.7780（+21.2%，p=0.0059），RMSE从0.0714降至0.0441（-38.2%，p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 该可扩展框架通过离线残差强化学习校正运动学误差，有效提升3D BCI运动想象解码性能，且无需额外神经数据，对神经康复、假肢控制和虚拟交互具有推进意义。

Abstract: Decoding continuous three-dimensional (3D) motor imagery (MI) using non-invasive electroencephalography (EEG)-based brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) remains challenging due to signal variability and residual decoding errors. Deep learning architectures such as convolutional neural network--long short-term memory (CNN--LSTM) models can capture spatial and temporal dynamics for continuous kinematic decoding; however, systematic residual errors persist in predicted trajectories. We propose a two-stage decoding framework that applies reinforcement learning (RL) to perform residual kinematic correction on the outputs of a CNN--LSTM decoder (CNN--LSTM--RL). The RL agent is trained offline without direct EEG input and instead operates on predicted kinematic trajectories to optimize movement accuracy relative to target trajectories. Decoding performance was quantified using Pearson correlation coefficients ($r$) and Root Mean Square Errors (RMSE) along the $x, y$, and $z$ axes. Compared to CNN--LSTM applied alone, CNN--LSTM--RL improved the mean correlation from $0.5076$ to $0.7181$ ($p = 0.0005$) in 2D and from $0.6420$ to $0.7780$ ($p = 0.0059$) in VR, with relative gains of $41.5\%$ and $21.2\%$, respectively. Correspondingly, RMSE was reduced from $0.0890$ to $0.0532$ (2D, $p < 0.0001$) and from $0.0714$ to $0.0441$ (VR, $p < 0.0001$), representing relative reductions of $40.2\%$ and $38.2\%$. These findings demonstrate that this scalable framework enhances 3D BCI MI decoding by correcting kinematic errors via offline residual RL without extra neural data, advancing neurorehabilitation, prosthetics, and virtual interaction.

</details>


### [279] [HCRMap: Pressure-Aware Hot-Expert Residency Mapping for 3.5D MoE Chiplet Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11586)
*Yongqin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HCRMap是一种面向3.5D多芯片系统的专家驻留映射框架，通过热专家副本的动态放置与复用，缓解MoE推理中计算、通信、内存及队列压力，相比现有方案实现43%-47%的端到端延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE大模型在推理时仅激活少量专家，但令牌路由导致专家热度严重倾斜——少数热专家持续接收大部分令牌，而其他专家负载极轻。在3.5D多芯片系统中，这种倾斜不仅造成计算不均衡，还会放大通信、内存带宽、I/O和执行队列的压力。核心问题不在于简单减少令牌移动，而在于跨不同内存层级动态放置和复用热专家副本。

Method: HCRMap根据专家热度、权重加载成本、迁移开销和运行时资源压力，动态决定哪些专家应被提升、保留、降级或驱逐，并将路由令牌组映射到合适的驻留副本，从而联合缓解通信、内存和队列瓶颈。

Result: 在预填充和解码阶段，HCRMap相比Hydra延迟降低43.6%和43.0%，相比MoEntwine降低34.5%和33.1%，相比PIMoE降低46.7%和46.0%。

Conclusion: HCRMap通过压力感知的专家副本管理，有效解决了MoE在3.5D系统中的热专家倾斜问题，显著提升了推理性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models (LLM) activate only a small number of experts during inference, but token routing introduces persistent expert hotness skew: a small set of hot experts continuously receives most tokens, while the remaining experts are lightly loaded. On 3.5D multi-chiplet systems, this skew not only causes compute imbalance but also amplifies pressure on communication, memory bandwidth, I/O, and execution queues. Therefore, the core problem is not simply to reduce token movement, but to dynamically place and reuse hot expert replicas across different memory tiers.
  This paper proposes HCRMap, a hot expert residency mapping framework for pressure-aware expert replica management in 3.5D MoE inference. Based on expert hotness, weight loading cost, migration overhead, and runtime resource pressure, HCRMap dynamically determines which experts should be promoted, retained, demoted, or evicted. It then maps routed token groups to suitable resident replicas, thereby jointly mitigating communication, memory, and queue bottlenecks.
  Experimental results show that HCRMap reduces end-to-end latency by 43.6% and 43.0% over Hydra in the prefill and decode stages, respectively; by 34.5% and 33.1% over MoEntwine; and by 46.7% and 46.0% over PIMoE.

</details>


### [280] [MAGIC: Transition-Aware Generation of Navigable Multi-Scene Game Worlds with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11594)
*Tsz Hei Fan,Choi Wing Fung,Yuxuan Wan,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGIC是一个四阶段流水线系统，能将自然语言提示转化为可运行的多场景游戏项目，解决了跨场景一致性、场景内可导航性和过渡评估三大难题，在100个多场景基准测试中实现了0.96的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代3D游戏的多场景导航关卡设计需要人工确保门户一致性、场景内可导航性及跨文件连通性，工作繁重。现有LLM/MLLM场景生成器只能单场景合成，无法通过简单重复生成连通的多场景世界。

Method: MAGIC采用四阶段流水线：(1) 从自然语言提示规划共享的过渡感知中间表示；(2) 使用洪泛填充验证器强制执行门户可达性来指定每个场景；(3) 生成场景及其过渡脚本；(4) 组合成一个完整项目。此外引入过渡聚焦评估代理用于实际运行测试过渡。

Result: 在100个多场景案例基准上，MAGIC为每个案例生成可执行项目，端到端过渡识别达到0.99精确率、0.95召回率和0.96 F1分数。逐阶段比较，比LLM基线和Holodeck恢复更多真实门户并生成更可导航的布局。

Conclusion: MAGIC有效解决了多场景游戏关卡自动生成的关键挑战，实现了从自然语言到可运行游戏项目的端到端转换，性能显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Multi-scene navigation (clearing an objective in one bounded space and then crossing a portal into the next) is a defining feature of contemporary 3D games, but authoring it is laborious: every portal must have consistent endpoints on both sides, each interior must remain navigable once it is furnished, and the resulting connectivity must be kept consistent across many files. Recent large language model (LLM) and multimodal LLM (MLLM) scene generators have made single-interior synthesis dramatically cheaper, yet they produce one scene at a time and cannot, by naive repetition, yield a connected multi-scene world. We identify three obstacles that single-scene methods leave unsolved: cross-scene consistency, in-scene navigability, and the evaluation of whether a transition actually works. We present MAGIC, a prompt-to-project system that addresses all three. MAGIC is a four-stage pipeline that turns a single natural-language prompt into a runnable multi-scene game project: it plans a shared transition-aware intermediate representation, specifies each scene while enforcing portal reachability with a flood-fill validator, generates the scenes together with their transition scripts, and combines them into one project. Because existing single-scene fidelity metrics never execute a transition, we further introduce a transition-focused evaluation agent that runs each transition in play. On a new benchmark of 100 multi-scene cases, MAGIC produces an executable project for every case and reaches 0.99 precision, 0.95 recall, and 0.96 F1 on end-to-end transition identification; stage by stage, it recovers more ground-truth portals and yields markedly more navigable layouts than an LLM baseline and Holodeck. Our code is available at https://github.com/sereneee1201/MAGIC/.

</details>


### [281] [Interaction Scaling: Grounding the Third Axis of Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11598)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出第三种扩展测试时计算的方法——交互（interaction），即模型生成产物、外部工具观察实际行为、模型据此修正。与推理延长和多次采样不同，交互通过引入真实观察打破了性能天花板。关键变量是"接地性"（grounding）：反馈和评估都必须来自真实观测。在编程任务上，交互策略持续提升而其他方法停滞；在视觉任务上，未接地的VLM评估会忽视明显缺陷，而接地工具能有效修复缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种扩展测试时计算的方法（推理延长和多次采样）存在根本局限：所有额外计算都来自相同的冻结权重和相同提示，无法引入模型不知道的新信息。作者希望探索第三种方式——交互，即模型与外部工具形成循环，每次迭代引入真实观察，从而突破前两种方法的天花板。

Method: 提出"接地性"（grounding）这一核心变量，要求反馈和评估都必须来自真实观测。在编程任务上，实现提出者-评审者（proposer-reviewer）框架；在视觉任务上，对比使用VLM作为评审者（未接地）与使用实际测量工具（接地）的效果差异。

Result: 编程任务：在固定token预算下，纯推理和最佳采样均出现平台期，而交互策略持续提升，提出者-评审者框架达到100%通过率且零方差，在三个模型家族上保持一致。视觉任务：未接地的VLM评审将15个明显有缺陷的视觉产物中的14个评为"完美"；接地工具能修复四个模态中40-74%的缺陷；同一VLM作为评审者会使幻灯片布局变差，而测量工具能修复它们。

Conclusion: 交互扩展是真实存在的，且不同于推理扩展和采样扩展。但只有当反馈和评估都接地时，交互扩展的效果才能显现出来。未接地的评估者会忽视可见缺陷，导致交互循环失效甚至恶化结果。

Abstract: There are two standard ways to spend more compute at test time: let a model reason longer, or sample more attempts and keep one. Both share a hidden limit: they are internal. Every extra token comes from the same frozen weights and the same prompt, so neither can tell the model anything it does not already know. We study a third way, interaction: the model proposes an artifact, an external instrument observes how it actually behaves, and the model revises. Each cycle imports a real observation, so interaction breaks through the ceiling the other two hit.
  We argue that a single variable governs this third axis, grounding, and that it must hold on both sides of the loop. The feedback that drives revision must come from an instrument that actually observes the flaw, and so must the metric that scores the result. On hard coding tasks at a fixed token budget, reasoning-only and best-of-N sampling both plateau (the latter even when an oracle picks the best sample), while every interaction strategy keeps improving; our proposer-reviewer harness reaches a perfect 100% pass rate with no run-to-run variance, and the gain holds across three model families. On rendered visual artifacts, the usual judge (a vision-language model, or VLM, reading a screenshot) rates 14 of 15 visibly broken figures "perfect," because the screenshot hides the flaws before the judge can see them. A tool that measures the real layout instead shows the loop removing 40-74% of defects across four modalities; and that same VLM, used as the reviewer, makes slide layouts worse where the measuring tool repairs them. Interaction scaling is real and distinct from reasoning and sampling, but only visible when both the feedback and the metric are grounded.

</details>


### [282] [Auditing the Risk Claims of Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11607)
*Hari Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 分布强化学习智能体声称的风险偏好（如CVaR）在多数情况下不可靠：在QR-DQN、C51、IQN及MinAtar/Atari环境中，40-95%的最强风险声明被统计审计驳斥，智能体学习的“风险”反映的是训练伪影而非环境随机性，且该伪影结构性存在、与最终分数无关、对种子特异；正对照实验验证审计方法有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习智能体输出的风险信息（如CVaR、一阶随机占优）被用于可解释性、风险敏感控制和安全监控，但这些风险声明的真实性和可靠性从未被直接量化审计过。该工作旨在填补这一空白，检验智能体的风险声称是否与真实环境风险一致。

Method: 提出审计框架：使用决策相关的筛选指标（top两个动作间的Wasserstein差距，等价于一阶随机占优违反的质量）、snapshot-restart蒙特卡洛作为真实风险基准、结合置换检验零假设、bootstrap反证和FDR控制的统计套件。在QR-DQN、C51、IQN三种分布强化学习算法上，对MinAtar（33个种子）和Atari（Breakout）进行系统审计，并设置正对照实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 40-95%的最强风险声明在95%置信水平下被驳斥；最强声明的排序与随机不可区分；几乎没有任何声明能被确认。该伪影在训练早期即已形成、与最终分数无关、对随机种子特异。在Atari Breakout上，预训练QR-DQN的所有top Breakout声明均被驳斥。正对照实验确认96-100%的真实声明被检出（相关性0.89-0.92）。基于头部CVaR建议的行动从有益到显著差于随机不等。训练风险或集成方法无法消除该伪影，重新校准只能通过使声明无效来通过审计。

Conclusion: 分布强化学习智能体学习到的“风险”本质上是训练伪影，而非对环境随机性的可靠表征，因此不应被直接用于风险敏感控制或安全监控。研究发布了审计工具包，并记录了两种在审计过程中曾产生误导性结果的隐性陷阱。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning agents learn full return distributions that are increasingly read at face value: for interpretability, risk-sensitive control, and safety monitoring. We ask a question theory anticipates but that has not been measured directly: are the risk claims of a trained distributional agent true? Our audit combines a decision-relevant screening metric (the excess Wasserstein gap between the top two actions, which equals the mass by which first-order stochastic dominance is violated), ground truth from snapshot-restart Monte Carlo, and a statistical harness (permutation nulls, bootstrap refutation, FDR control) without which the audit itself manufactures false conclusions. Across QR-DQN, C51, and IQN on MinAtar (33 runs), 40-95% of the strongest claimed risk trade-offs are refuted at 95% confidence, the placement of the strongest claims is statistically indistinguishable from truth-blind, and essentially no claim is confirmable: for these agents, the learned "risk" reflects a training artifact rather than environment stochasticity. The artifact is structural (fully formed early in training, uncorrelated with final score, idiosyncratic to each seed) and appears unchanged at full-Atari scale, with every top Breakout claim of a pretrained near-state-of-the-art QR-DQN refuted. Positive controls of known magnitude confirm 96-100% of real claims (correlation 0.89-0.92): the reading measures the agents, not the audit. Acting on the heads' CVaR advice at their most-flagged states ranges from beneficial to significantly worse than chance. Neither training for risk nor ensembling removes the artifact, and recalibration passes the audit only by nullifying the claims: the head is uninformative, not merely miscalibrated. We release the toolkit and document two silent pitfalls that produced convincing but wrong audits of our own.

</details>


### [283] [Lesioned Multimodal Language Models Reproduce Aphasic Picture-Naming Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11621)
*Yong Yang,Xiang Guan,Sophie Arheix-Parras,Saeed Ahmadi,Roger Newman-Norlund,Leonardo Bonilha,Christopher Rorden,Julius Fridriksson,Rutvik H. Desai,Srihari Nelakuditi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过扰动多模态语言模型LLaVA 1.6，成功再现了卒中后失语症患者在图片命名中的个体化错误模式（七类错误中的六类），并能在至少六个类别上匹配97.8%患者的个体错误轮廓，在全部七个类别上匹配79.5%的患者，为构建失语症数字孪生提供了定量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验通用语言模型（非专为临床模拟设计）是否能够复现卒中后失语症患者特征性的命名错误模式，为建立失语症的计算机模型提供基础。

Method: 使用LLaVA 1.6多模态语言模型，通过对不同层、不同比例和不同噪声量进行扰动配置，模拟278名失语症患者在费城命名测试中的反应，并使用验证过的神经分类器将反应分为七类。

Result: 六类错误（正确、语义、混合、无关、新词、无反应）在特定参数空间区域以临床可比比例出现；97.8%的患者在至少六类中、79.5%的患者在全部七类中匹配了个体错误轮廓；蒙特卡洛基线验证了匹配反映的是联合跨类别结构而非边际重叠。

Conclusion: 建立了再现个体失语症图片命名错误模式的定量框架，表明语言模型有潜力作为卒中后失语症患者的数字孪生。

Abstract: Aphasia following stroke commonly produces systematic naming errors with characteristic profiles, but whether general-purpose language models not designed for clinical simulation can reproduce these patterns remains untested. We investigated (1) whether lesions or controlled perturbations to a multimodal language model can reproduce different types of errors in picture naming, and (2) whether the framework can reproduce the complete error profile of individual persons with aphasia (PWAs). Using LLaVA 1.6, we evaluated perturbation configurations that varied the layer, proportion, and amount of noise applied to model units. We examined 278 PWAs on the Philadelphia Naming Test, classifying responses into seven categories using a validated neural classifier. Six of seven response categories (correct, semantic, mixed, unrelated, neologism, no response errors) emerged at clinically-comparable proportions across distinct parameter space regions, with formal paraphasia being the exception. Searching the perturbation space revealed configurations that reproduced the individual error profile in at least six of seven categories for 97.8% of PWAs and in all seven categories for 79.5% of PWAs. Monte Carlo baselines confirmed that this matching reflects joint inter-category structure rather than marginal overlap. These results establish a quantitative framework for reproducing individual aphasic error patterns in picture naming. They suggest the potential for language models to serve as digital twins of individuals with post-stroke aphasia.

</details>


### [284] [Reproducing human biases in route choice using large language models: Toward scalable behavioral modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11632)
*Jiangtao Han,Shoufeng Ma,Shuxian Xu,Geng Li,Shuai Ling,Ning Jia,Zhengbing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）能否在没有明确设定前景理论参数的情况下，再现人类在路径选择等决策中的非理性行为偏差。实验表明，LLM能够产生与前景理论一致的行为模式，为大规模智能体建模和行为模拟提供了可扩展的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 累积前景理论（CPT）能有效刻画人类选择中的系统性行为偏差，但其大规模应用（如仿真和智能体建模）依赖于个体层面的CPT参数标定。传统方法依赖调查和受控实验，难以泛化且无法捕捉人类决策的多样性。因此，需要探索一种可扩展的方式来模拟人类的非理性决策行为。

Method: 以路径选择为代表性场景，设计行为评估框架，系统比较LLM生成的决策与CPT预测的人类行为模式，检验LLM能否再现非理性选择偏差。

Result: LLM能够再现人类的非理性选择偏差，并在不确定性下展现出与前景理论效应一致的决策行为。

Conclusion: 生成式AI模型可能为模拟人类决策过程提供可扩展的替代方案，为下一代大规模智能体仿真和AI驱动的行为研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Human choice behavior, including route choice, exhibits systematic behavioral biases that deviate from the assumptions of full rationality. Cumulative prospect theory (CPT) has been widely recognized as an effective framework for characterizing such behavioral patterns. However, its large-scale application, particularly in simulation and agent-based modeling, critically depends on specifying individual-level CPT parameters, which remain a major bottleneck. Conventional approaches typically rely on surveys and controlled experiments to calibrate CPT parameters, yet these methods are difficult to generalize and often fail to capture the full diversity of human decision-making. To address this challenge, this paper investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can reproduce human behavioral biases in choice-making without explicit specification of prospect-theoretic parameters. Using route choice as a representative scenario, we design a behavioral evaluation framework and systematically compare LLM-generated decisions with established human behavioral patterns predicted by CPT. Experimental results demonstrate that LLMs are capable of reproducing non-rational human choice biases and can exhibit decision behaviors consistent with prospect-theoretic effects under uncertainty. These findings suggest that generative AI models may provide a scalable alternative for modeling human decision processes and offer a promising foundation for next-generation large-scale agent-based simulation and AI-driven behavioral research.

</details>


### [285] [Think Through a Bottleneck: Hourglass Reasoning for Rigorous Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11696)
*Huan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"沙漏推理"（Hourglass reasoning）框架，通过严格隔离推理阶段、仅允许压缩符号状态（规则T和模式φ）在阶段间传递，显著提升大语言模型在少样本归纳推理中的表现。在ARC-AGI-2、ChipBench和BBEH-Linguini三个基准上验证了有效性，消融实验证明收益来自阶段间信息隔离而非提示措辞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我精炼方法（如让模型显式表达推断规则）对强化少样本归纳推理作用甚微，需要探索真正影响推理质量的信息流机制。

Method: 提出Hourglass推理框架：冻结的LLM作为元构造器，为每个任务构建符号编码器-解码器。包含五个模块——归纳（Induction）将支持示例压缩为模式φ和临时支架z；演绎（Deduction）从这些信息中推导规则T并丢弃z；实现者（Implementer）将(φ,T)编译为工件；错误驱动的精炼器（Refiner）修正(φ,T)并从头重新生成工件。仅(φ,T)跨越阶段边界。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-2上，最佳5次准确率比迭代精炼基线提升14个百分点；在ChipBench上，使用GPT-5.5时将Verilog综合准确率从31%提升至58%（接近翻倍）；在BBEH-Linguini（国际语言学奥林匹克谜题）上，逆转了显式语言化损害性能的倾向。

Conclusion: 驱动冻结LLM归纳推理的关键是信息在推理过程中的流动方式（阶段间严格隔离，仅传递压缩符号状态），而非表达所使用的语言或提示措辞。

Abstract: Self-refinement often fails to strengthen few-shot inductive reasoning in large language models. Prompting a model to explicitly state its inferred rule does little on its own. What actually matters is a structurally enforced isolation between reasoning stages, so that information can only pass between them as a compressed symbolic state.
  We introduce \textbf{Hourglass reasoning}, which enforces strict context isolation between reasoning stages. The frozen LLM acts as a meta-constructor, building for each task a symbolic encoder--decoder: an Induction module compresses the support examples into a schema $φ$ (encoder) and a transient scaffold $z$; a Deduction module derives rule $T$ (decoder) from these and discards $z$; an Implementer compiles $(φ, T)$ into artifacts; an error-driven Refiner revises $(φ, T)$ and regenerates artifacts from scratch. Only $(φ, T)$ crosses stage boundaries, so all refinement stays anchored to the rule.
  We evaluate Hourglass across three benchmarks spanning visual abstraction, hardware synthesis, and textual rule induction, using GPT-5.5 and Gemini 3.1 Pro. On ARC-AGI-2, it raises best-of-5 accuracy by up to 14 points over an iterative-refinement baseline. On ChipBench, it nearly doubles Verilog synthesis accuracy with GPT-5.5, from 31\% to 58\%. BBEH-Linguini draws on puzzles from the International Linguistics Olympiad, a setting where prior work has shown that explicit verbalization can hurt performance. Hourglass mitigates this tendency, and on Gemini 3.1 Pro, it reverses the effect entirely.
  Ablations confirm that these gains come from the isolation between stages and the quality of the initial induction, not from prompt wording or the particular symbolic form used. It is how information flows through the reasoning process, rather than the language used to express it, that drives inductive reasoning in frozen LLMs.

</details>


### [286] [Playful AI in Professional Email: A Field Experiment on Tone and Recipient Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11749)
*Ziv Ben-Zion,Teddy Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过随机交叉现场实验（121名员工，16,880封邮件）发现，GPT-5改写邮件可改变情感基调，但不直接影响打开率、回复率或响应时间；而发件人自身的情感积极性则显著预测收件人行为，构成AI编辑影响行为的间接路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI辅助写作是否以及通过何种机制改变收件人的实际行为，填补AI沟通效果因果证据的空白。

Method: 随机交叉现场实验：121名员工在三周内分别经历三种条件（无辅助、GPT-5改写为幽默语气、GPT-5改写为专业语气），分析16,880封工作邮件的收件人行为数据。

Result: 幽默改写提升情感积极性（B=+0.068, p<0.001），专业改写降低情感积极性（B=-0.041, p<0.001）；两种改写均无直接显著行为效果；但发件人自身情感积极性显著预测打开（OR=2.05）和回复（OR=3.32, p<0.001），构成间接路径。

Conclusion: AI辅助沟通并非通过其使用本身，而是通过改变语言的情感基调间接影响职场参与行为。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly reshaping workplace communication, yet whether AI-assisted writing changes how recipients actually behave, and through what channel, remains unknown. Here, in a randomized crossover field experiment, 121 employees across six companies sent work emails under three conditions over three weeks: unaided writing, GPT-5 rewriting in a playful tone, and GPT-5 rewriting in a professional tone. Across 16,880 emails, playful editing increased emotional positivity (B=+0.068, p<0.001), and professional editing decreased it (B=-0.041, p<0.001), yet neither condition directly altered open rates, reply rates, or response times. Instead, within-sender positivity strongly predicted both opening (OR=2.05) and replying (OR=3.32, p<0.001), a significant indirect pathway through which AI editing shaped behavior, in the absence of any direct effect. These findings suggest that AI-assisted communication shapes workplace engagement not through its use, but through the emotional tone of the language it produces.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [An LLM-powered Agentic Recommendation System for Connected TV Content Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09988)
*Lei Shi,Di Wang,Harry Tran,Helsing Xu,Yuchen Lu,Dhara Ghodasara,Wilson Chaney,Xueting Liao,Jerry Yu,Huayu Ding,Mingze Gao,Shike Mei,Shuo Tang,Zhe Zhang,Jianming He,Abhishek Kumar,Haotian Wu,Hamed Firooz,Li Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的智能体推荐系统，用于Connected TV内容发现。该系统利用大语言模型的推理能力处理异构上下文信号，并采用智能体架构协调LLM和传统ML组件，重点解决了LLM在推荐系统中的推理延迟等实际限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统（从多阶段架构到统一生成式架构）难以灵活整合多样化的上下文信号（如热门话题、突发新闻、文化事件、跨平台用户活动等），因为这些系统设计用于处理结构化的行为信号，缺乏处理非结构化或异构格式信息的推理能力。手工整合这些信号需要大量特征工程、定制化数据管道和精心调优的启发式规则。

Method: 提出了一种LLM驱动的智能体推荐系统架构，该架构：(1) 利用LLM的推理能力自然处理和合成不同模式与结构的多样信号；(2) 采用智能体架构编排专用组件，使每个子任务由最适合的方法（LLM或传统ML）处理；(3) 重点工程化克服了LLM在推荐中的实际局限，特别是推理延迟问题。

Result: 主要贡献在于工程方法上成功克服了LLM用于推荐系统的实际限制（尤其是推理延迟），分享了构建结合LLM灵活性与传统推荐技术性能的混合系统的经验教训和权衡。

Conclusion: 混合系统架构（LLM+传统ML）是当前阶段平衡推荐系统灵活性与性能的有效方案，通过智能体编排可以充分发挥各自优势，但需要在延迟、精度和可扩展性等方面进行实际权衡。

Abstract: Recommendation systems, from traditional multi-stage to recent unified generative architectures, face challenges in incorporating diverse contextual signals, such as trending topics, breaking news, cultural events, and cross-surface user activities, into their ranking pipelines. These systems are designed to consume structured behavioral signals with consistent schemas, and lack the reasoning capability to naturally process unstructured or heterogeneously formatted contextual information. Incorporating such signals typically requires feature engineering, bespoke data pipelines, and carefully tuned heuristics. In this paper, we present an LLM-powered agentic recommendation system designed for Connected TV (CTV) content discovery that addresses these limitations. Our system leverages the reasoning capabilities of large language models to naturally process and synthesize diverse signals across varying schemas and structures, eliminating much of the manual integration inherent in traditional ranking and retrieval systems. Recognizing that current LLM-based solutions still fall short of traditional machine learning models in several recommendation tasks, including retrieval efficiency, personalization precision, and scalability, we adopt an agentic architecture that orchestrates specialized components, allowing each sub-task to be handled by the most suitable method, whether LLM-based or traditional ML. The main contribution of this work is our engineering approach to successfully overcoming the practical limitations of enabling LLM for recommendation, particularly inference latency. We share insights from our work and discuss the trade-offs and lessons learned in building a hybrid system that combines the flexibility of LLMs with the performance of established recommendation techniques.

</details>


### [288] [Tokenizing Numerical and Embedding Features for LLM RecSys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10016)
*Zhe Xu,Ankit Peshin,Chiyu Zhang,Feng Qi,Johnson Lui,Anil Ramakrishna,Justin Johnson,Carl Hu,Kaushik Rangadurai,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出软令牌融合框架，将数值和嵌入特征映射到LLM嵌入空间，使异构推荐信号能通过标准令牌接口被LLM消费，实验表明该方法在Amazon推荐基准上优于LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统主要处理离散文本令牌，无法利用实际推荐流程中连续的数值特征和稠密嵌入特征，导致细粒度非文本信号难以被有效利用。

Method: 提出软令牌融合框架，将数值和嵌入特征映射到LLM嵌入空间；在共享参数的LLM双塔检索模型中实例化，引入基于交互的融合模块以在插入LLM输入前细化嵌入和数值软令牌。

Result: 在三个Amazon推荐基准上，软令牌融合方法比LLM基线检索性能更好，且基于交互的融合比直接拼接异构软令牌更有效。

Conclusion: 软令牌融合框架能有效桥接LLM推荐系统与异构推荐信号之间的鸿沟，交互式融合策略优于简单的拼接方式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as backbone architectures for recommender systems because of their strong sequence modeling and representation learning capabilities. However, most LLM-based recommenders operate primarily on discrete textual tokens, whereas practical recommendation pipelines also rely on continuous numerical features and dense embedding features produced by upstream feature engineering or pretrained encoders. This mismatch limits the ability of LLM-based models to exploit fine-grained non-textual signals. We propose a soft-token fusion framework that maps numerical and embedding features into the LLM embedding space, allowing heterogeneous recommendation signals to be consumed through the standard token interface. We instantiate the framework in a shared-parameter LLM-based two-tower retrieval model and introduce an interaction-based fusion module that refines embedding and numerical soft tokens before they are inserted into the final LLM input. Experiments on three Amazon recommendation benchmarks show that soft-token fusion improves retrieval performance over LLM-based baselines, and that interaction-based fusion is more effective than direct concatenation of heterogeneous soft tokens.

</details>


### [289] [The Effect of Multi-Lingual and Keyword Adversarial Injection on LLM Relevance Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10080)
*Nguyen Khoi Vo,Duy Duong Tuong,Oleg Zendel,Mark Sanderson*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究了跨语言提示注入攻击对LLM作为相关性评估工具的影响，发现多语言查询注入可有效操纵评分并规避现有防御机制，表明语言泛化可作为攻击向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作信息检索中的自动相关性评估器，但其在对抗性操作下的鲁棒性，特别是在多语言环境下的鲁棒性，尚不充分了解。

Method: 使用TREC Deep Learning数据集和两种开源模型，在8种不同资源水平的语言中，分别实施基于指令和基于内容的注入攻击策略，并评估现有防御机制的缓解效果。

Result: 多语言查询注入能有效提升相关性评分，并能规避现有提示注入防御；即使修改防御机制，注入攻击也能轻易适应并绕过。

Conclusion: 当前防御方法存在关键漏洞，语言泛化可作为攻击向量，需要更鲁棒和主动的评估框架来保障LLM-as-a-judge系统的安全。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being used as automated judges
  for relevance evaluation in information retrieval, yet their robustness to
  adversarial manipulation remains insufficiently understood, particularly in
  multilingual settings. In this work, we investigate the impact of
  cross-lingual prompt injection attacks on LLM-based relevance judgments using
  TREC Deep Learning collections and two open-weight models under established
  prompting frameworks. We examine both instruction-based and content-based
  injection strategies in 8 languages spanning different resource levels. Our
  results demonstrate that multilingual query-based injections are highly
  effective in inflating relevance scores while simultaneously evading existing
  prompt-injection defenses. We further found that, although existing defense
  mechanisms can be modified to mitigate such attacks, these injections can be
  easily adapted to bypass them. These findings highlight a critical gap in
  current defense approaches and demonstrate that language generalization can
  act as an attack vector, underscoring the need for more robust and proactive
  evaluation frameworks for LLM-as-a-judge systems.

</details>


### [290] [Scaling and Stabilizing Large-Scale Embedding-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10096)
*Zhen Yang,Juexin Lin,Hongwei Shang,Kaihao Li,Feng Liu,Satya Chembolu,Xunfan Cai,Xinyi Liu,Cun Mu,Tony Lee,Ciya Liao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套在Walmart部署的统一检索管线，通过混合硬负样本挖掘和旧模型知识蒸馏两个核心贡献，解决了双编码器模型的训练-推理差距和模型升级中的知识丢失问题，在NDCG@5上提升7.34%，总收入提升0.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准双编码器模型存在训练-推理差距（训练时仅在小规模候选池上优化，推理时需区分数亿商品），且替换为高容量骨干模型会导致领域特定知识丢失和检索行为不一致。

Method: (1) 混合硬负样本挖掘：结合在线跨批次采样（增加负样本多样性一个数量级）和混合离线挖掘（交叉编码器预测+元数据启发式）。(2) 旧模型感知蒸馏：从DistilBERT迁移至GTE-base编码器，采用Warm-Start蒸馏技术将旧模型的领域知识迁移至新骨干。

Result: 离线实验和在线A/B测试验证有效，已部署至生产环境，NDCG@5提升7.34%，总收入提升0.50%。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一管线同时解决了训练信号质量和模型演化两大问题，在大规模电商搜索中取得了显著且可落地的业务收益。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval (EBR) is foundational to large-scale e-commerce search, yet its effectiveness is often constrained by the quality of training signals and the representational capacity of the encoder. Standard dual-encoders suffer from a training-inference gap: they are optimized on narrow candidate pools but must discriminate against hundreds of millions of items during inference. Furthermore, while transitioning to higher-capacity backbones can mitigate this gap, simply replacing a mature model can lead to inconsistent retrieval behavior and a loss of the domain-specific knowledge established in previous iterations. In this paper, we present a unified pipeline deployed at Walmart that addresses both signal quality and model evolution. Our contributions are two-fold: (1) Hybrid Hard Negative Mining: We integrate Online Cross-Batch Sampling to increase negative diversity by an order of magnitude and Hybrid Offline Mining, which combines cross-encoder predictions with metadata heuristics to identify nuanced mismatches. (2) Legacy-Aware Distillation: We transition from DistilBERT to a higher-capacity GTE-base encoder. To ensure a smooth and superior transition, we introduce a Warm-Start Distillation technique that transfers domain-specific expertise from the legacy model to the new backbone. Validated through extensive offline experiments and online A/B testing, the proposed pipeline is deployed in live production, delivering a +7.34% improvement in NDCG@5 and a +0.50% lift in gross revenue.

</details>


### [291] [Adaptive Model Compression (AMC): Saliency-Driven Resource Allocation for Ultra-Low-Power Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10109)
*Jiayin Hu,Kai Yuan,Vanessa Hu,Xuetao Yin,Jianhua Li,Sean Suchter*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出自适应模型压缩框架AMC，基于token重要性动态分配硬件资源，在边缘设备上实现59.2%能耗降低和2.24倍吞吐量提升，仅牺牲3.6%精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer模型在资源受限的边缘设备上部署困难，传统静态推理对简单和复杂token采用相同计算强度，导致高能耗和内存开销。

Method: 提出自适应模型压缩框架，通过多层级架构识别高显著性token进行全精度处理，同时对低显著性数据激进降低秩和位宽。

Result: 在45nm CMOS硬件上实现59.2%系统能耗降低和2.24倍吞吐量提升，仅带来3.6%精度损失。

Conclusion: AMC通过按需使用高精度计算，有效延长移动设备电池寿命，在保持稳健性能的同时实现显著能效提升。

Abstract: Deploying large-scale transformer models on resource-constrained edge devices remains a challenge due to the high energy and memory overhead inherent in static inference, which processes simple and complex tokens with uniform intensity. To address this, we propose Adaptive Model Compression (AMC), a saliency-driven framework that dynamically allocates hardware resources based on token importance. By implementing a multi-tier architecture, our system identifies critical high-saliency information for full-precision processing while aggressively reducing the rank and bit-width of less significant data. Experimental results demonstrate that AMC achieves a 59.2% reduction in system energy and a 2.24x increase in throughput on 45nm CMOS hardware. This approach effectively extends the battery life of mobile devices by utilizing high-definition compute only where necessary, maintaining robust performance with a marginal 3.6% accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [292] [MC-RAG System: A Structure-Driven RAG System for Multi-Constraint Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10151)
*Xiao Zhang,Yang Wan,Yi Li,Miao Xie,Chunli Lv*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MC-RAG，一种将检索增强生成转化为知识图谱子图匹配问题的结构驱动RAG系统，用于处理多约束查询，通过语义与结构嵌入及路径级索引实现可解释、结构感知且约束一致的检索与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在处理多约束复杂查询时，常出现约束违反、事实不一致或幻觉问题，缺乏对查询中多重约束的结构化理解和一致性保障。

Method: 将检索过程重新定义为知识图谱上的子图匹配问题，融合语义嵌入与结构嵌入，结合路径级索引，实现结构感知的约束一致性检索与生成。

Result: 构建了端到端交互式可解释RAG演示系统，支持医学和百科类多约束查询的可视化解析、结构匹配与答案生成。

Conclusion: MC-RAG通过结构驱动的检索范式有效提升了多约束查询下的检索准确性和生成一致性，并提供了可解释的RAG流程。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are widely adopted in question answering, yet they often fail to satisfy complex multi-constraint queries, leading to constraint violations, factual inconsistencies, or hallucinations. We present Structure-Driven RAG System for Multi-Constraint Queries(MC-RAG), a structure-driven RAG system that reformulates retrieval as a subgraph matching problem over a knowledge graph. By integrating semantic and structural embeddings with path-level indexing, MC-RAG performs interpretable, structure-aware, and constraint-consistent retrieval and generation. During the demonstration, participants can input medical or encyclopedic multi-constraint queries, visualize how the system parses constraints, performs structural matching, and generates answers, thereby experiencing an end-to-end, interactive, and explainable RAG pipeline. A demo video is available at https://youtu.be/J8kahzmAnu0.

</details>


### [293] [From Patent Expiry to Business Pathways: AI Workflows for Activating Innovation Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10179)
*Sidney Shapiro,Mark Price*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI框架，用于发现过期或失效的专利，识别技术趋势，并将专利披露转化为商业路径（如SaaS产品、许可包、咨询方案等）。该框架将专利失效视为商业信号和档案转变，而非单纯的法律问题。系统结合了专利元数据、维护费记录、法律状态、语义搜索、专利族分析、市场信号和生成式AI。在CIPO ST.96的378条记录上验证了可复现的摄入、稳定排名和符合模式的输出，同时暴露了法律状态覆盖不全的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利数据库是最大的公开技术知识库之一，但大量过期或失效专利中的技术知识难以被发现、解读和再利用，造成了知识浪费。

Method: 提出了一个AI驱动的框架，包含专利元数据、维护费记录、法律状态指标、语义搜索、专利族分析、市场信号和生成式AI工作流。采用透明评分模型进行候选筛选，并使用本地部署的Qwen3.6模型生成结构化审查包。

Result: 在CIPO ST.96周报的378条记录上进行了概念验证，识别出20个过期、失效或即将失效的候选专利。实验证明了可复现的数据摄入、权重扰动下的稳定排名以及符合模式的模型输出，同时也暴露了法律状态覆盖不完整的问题。

Conclusion: AI可以作为休眠技术知识的发现和翻译层，但此类系统必须明确表示法律不确定性、数据局限性和商业化风险，并且需要注册簿审查和专家复核来弥补不足。

Abstract: Patent databases represent one of the largest public archives of technical knowledge, yet much of this knowledge remains difficult to identify, interpret, and reuse once patent rights expire or lapse. This paper proposes an AI-enabled framework for discovering expired and lapsing patents, identifying technology trends, and translating patent disclosures into business pathways. We use pathways to mean structured commercialization routes such as SaaS products, services, licensing packages, consulting playbooks, training offerings, data products, or internal process tools. The framework treats patent expiry as both a business signal and an archival transition, not primarily as a legal problem. Legal status remains important, but it is one risk-screening input alongside customer need, implementation feasibility, channel access, and market timing. We describe a system architecture that combines patent metadata, maintenance-fee records, legal-status indicators, semantic search, patent-family analysis, market signals, and generative AI workflows. A proof of concept parses all 378 records in an official weekly CIPO ST.96 archive, identifies 20 expired, lapsed, or near-expiry candidates, tests the stability of the transparent scoring model, and uses a locally hosted Qwen3.6 model to populate structured review packets. The evaluation demonstrates reproducible ingestion, stable rankings under weight perturbation, and schema-conformant model output, while also exposing incomplete legal-status coverage and the need for register and expert review. We argue that AI can function as a discovery and translation layer for dormant technical knowledge, but that such systems must explicitly represent legal uncertainty, data limitations, and commercialization risk.

</details>


### [294] [Multilingual Semantic Retrieval for Apple Music Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10239)
*Vishalaksh Aggarwal,Kevin Sebastian,Vivek Kanojiya,Leo Le,Nick Tucey,Santosh Shankar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 苹果音乐构建了一个基于3.05亿参数多语言双编码器的语义检索系统，通过混合检索架构融合密集向量与现有词元索引，在全球A/B测试中实现整体转化率提升2.28%，尾部查询转化率提升7.93%，无结果率降低86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对覆盖150+商店、数十种语言、每日新增数十万曲目的超大规模音乐检索场景，拼写错误、音译和跨语言查询导致的搜索召回问题成为影响会话质量的关键因素，尤其是占独特查询大多数的尾部查询。

Method: 采用3.05亿参数的Siamese双编码器（基于GTE-multilingual-base微调），通过课程调度的多目标训练策略进行优化；集成到混合检索架构中，利用分位数分布匹配将稠密近邻结果与现有基于词元的索引融合，无需重新训练下游排序器即可部署。

Result: 离线：Hit@10相对提升69%；全球在线A/B测试：整体转化率提升2.28%，无结果率降低86%，尾部查询转化率提升7.93%（中频0.89%，高频0.14%），所有商店均有增益且无退化。

Conclusion: 语义检索在不干扰已良好服务的热门查询的前提下，显著改善了困难查询的召回效果，是该平台规模最大的搜索质量改进之一。

Abstract: Apple Music serves listeners across 150+ storefronts in dozens of languages, with a catalog that grows by hundreds of thousands of new tracks daily. At this scale, search recall on misspelled, transliterated, and cross-lingual queries becomes a dominant driver of session quality, particularly for tail queries that account for the majority of unique queries. We present a multilingual semantic retrieval system built on a 305M-parameter Siamese bi-encoder fine-tuned from GTE-multilingual-base with curriculum-scheduled multi-objective training. The model is integrated into the search stack via a hybrid retrieval architecture that blends dense nearest-neighbor results with the existing token-based index using quantile distribution matching, enabling deployment without retraining downstream rankers. Offline, the model achieves a 69% relative improvement in Hit@10 over GTE-multilingual-base. In a worldwide online A/B test, the system delivers a 2.28% relative conversion-rate (CR) lift overall, an 86% reduction in the no-result rate, and gains across every storefront with no observed regressions. The improvement is concentrated where it is needed most: tail queries see a 7.93% relative CR lift, compared with 0.89% for mid-frequency queries and 0.14% for head queries -- evidence that semantic retrieval improves recall on hard queries without disturbing well-served popular ones. To our knowledge, this is one of the largest search-quality improvements deployed on the platform.

</details>


### [295] [SVD-RAG: Efficient Tree-Organized Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Singular Value Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10316)
*Zhihui Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SVD-RAG提出用奇异值分解（SVD）替代LLM摘要，对稠密句子嵌入矩阵进行抽取式摘要，构建层次化RAG树结构，在保持检索质量接近RAPTOR（MRR 0.867 vs 0.875）的同时，建树速度提升317倍（0.1s vs 31.7s），token消耗减少约85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAPTOR虽能通过层级树结构提升RAG检索效率，但其依赖LLM进行抽象式摘要，计算成本高昂，难以大规模部署。现有方法缺乏确定性、成本过高且难以自适应内容复杂度。

Method: 对稠密句子嵌入矩阵进行SVD分解，利用主成分的能量贡献识别最具信息量的句子，通过能量比阈值τ自适应控制压缩程度，实现抽取式摘要，替代RAPTOR中的LLM抽象式摘要。

Result: 与RAPTOR对比：MRR 0.867 vs 0.875（差距1-5%），Recall@1 0.483 vs 0.458；建树速度317倍提升（0.1s vs 31.7s）；token消耗减少约85%。在205个chunk、100个查询的多主题基准上，Recall@1提升4.2倍，MRR提升3.1倍。

Conclusion: SVD-RAG是一种确定性、低成本、内容自适应的层次化RAG方法，通过SVD抽取式摘要大幅降低计算开销，同时保持与LLM摘要相当的检索质量，适合大规模部署。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems enhance large language models by retrieving relevant documents from external knowledge bases. Recent work by Sarthi et al. (2024) introduced RAPTOR, which organizes documents into hierarchical tree structures for efficient retrieval, but requires expensive LLM-based abstractive summarization at each internal node -- making large-scale deployment prohibitively costly.
  We present SVD-RAG, the first method to apply Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) on dense sentence embedding matrices for extractive summarization in hierarchical RAG. Unlike classical LSA which operates on sparse TF-IDF matrices, SVD-RAG exploits the rich semantic representations of modern embedding models, identifying the most informative sentences through their energy contribution in the principal components. Our approach is (1) deterministic -- unlike LLM-based summarization, SVD produces identical results for the same input; (2) cost-efficient -- tree construction requires no additional API calls beyond the initial embedding, reducing token consumption by ~85%; and (3) content-adaptive -- the energy-ratio threshold tau automatically adjusts compression based on content complexity. In a controlled head-to-head comparison using identical corpora, clustering, and beam search, SVD-RAG achieves retrieval quality within 1-5% of RAPTOR with LLM summarization (MRR 0.867 vs. 0.875, Recall@1 0.483 vs. 0.458) while building the tree 317x faster (0.1s vs. 31.7s). On a scaled multi-topic benchmark with 205 chunks and 100 queries across 20 topic variations, SVD-RAG achieves a 4.2x improvement in Recall@1 and 3.1x improvement in MRR over flat embedding retrieval. We provide a detailed cost analysis and parameter sensitivity study. Our implementation is released as an open-source Python package.

</details>


### [296] [Implicit Fine-tuning via Context Engineering: A Curriculum Learning Framework for Multimodal Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10532)
*Yunpeng Hong,Chenyang Bu,Di Wu,Yi He,Xindong Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上证明了多模态实体对齐（MMEA）中上下文工程与模型微调之间的数学等价性，并提出了基于课程学习的PTFEA框架，将微调策略转化为可解释的上下文工程，在五个公开数据集上取得了优异性能，同时大幅降低了计算开销（运行时间从21小时降至1小时，token消耗减少80%以上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMEA方法依赖黑盒上下文工程策略，受限于LLM参数容量且缺乏理论可解释性。

Method: 首先理论验证了MMEA任务中上下文工程与模型微调的数学等价性；然后提出PTFEA框架：(1) 自适应难度调制，利用置信度阈值动态调整信息注入阶段；(2) 三阶段渐进推理，从简单到复杂逐步融入实体信息。

Result: 在五个公开数据集上超越强基线；在ICWIKI上，将Qwen2.5-72B与14B的H@1差距缩小至0.6%；相比MM-ChatAlign，运行时间从21小时降至1小时，token消耗从2200-3000降至200-400。

Conclusion: 为MMEA中统一上下文工程与微调提供了首个理论框架，为未来将更多微调策略转化为上下文工程范式铺平了道路。

Abstract: Multimodal Entity Alignment (MMEA) aims to identify equivalent entities across different modalities. While existing methods enhance MMEA performance through black-box context engineering strategies, their reliance on LLM parameter capacity and lack of theoretical interpretability remain unresolved. To this end, we first theoretically validate the mathematical equivalence between context engineering and model fine-tuning in MMEA tasks, demonstrating that prompt components simulate contrastive learning-based sequential fine-tuning in MMEA. Building on this foundation, we then propose PTFEA, a curriculum-learning-inspired framework that translates fine-tuning strategies into interpretable context engineering. Specifically, adaptive difficulty modulation dynamically adjusts information injection stages using confidence thresholds, establishing mathematical equivalence between curriculum learning weights and context sample selection; and three-stage progressive inference incorporates entity information from simple to complex cases, mirroring the gradient descent process in fine-tuning. Experiments on five public datasets demonstrate that PTFEA consistently outperforms strong baselines. In particular, on the ICWIKI dataset, PTFEA narrows the H@1 gap between Qwen2.5-72B and 14B to 0.6%. Moreover, compared with the representative context-engineering-based MMEA method MM-ChatAlign, PTFEA reduces the runtime of Qwen2.5-72B from 21 hours to 1 hour and lowers token consumption from 2200-3000 to 200-400, achieving over 80% reduction on the ICWIKI dataset. This work provides the first theoretical framework unifying context engineering and fine-tuning in MMEA, paving the way for future research that seeks to translate additional fine-tuning strategies into context engineering paradigms. Our code is available at https://github.com/DMiC-Lab-HFUT/PTFEA.

</details>


### [297] [RecRec: Recursive Refinement for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10541)
*Pervez Shaik,Prosenjit Biswas,Abhinav Thorat,Ravi Kolla,Niranjan Pedanekar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RecRec提出了一种递归推理视角的序列推荐方法，通过维护紧凑的潜在状态并利用证据锚定校正机制进行递归更新，仅用3.9M-14M参数即可匹配或超越现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐系统通常采用单遍编码交互历史的方式，依赖不断加深的网络架构来捕捉复杂模式，缺乏迭代精炼过程。本文从递归推理视角出发，探索能否将用户偏好建模为可递归精炼的持久潜在状态。

Method: 提出RecRec模型，维护一个紧凑的潜在状态，通过共享递归模块基于交互证据进行更新。核心创新是证据锚定校正机制（evidence-anchored correction mechanism），将每次更新锚定在原始交互上下文中，防止深度递归推理中的语义漂移。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，RecRec以3.9M-14M参数匹配或超越SOTA序列、图以及推理增强推荐模型。消融实验表明递归精炼和证据锚定校正门控均对性能有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 递归潜在推理可作为更深层或语言模型架构的可扩展替代方案，验证了递归精炼在序列推荐中的有效性。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems typically infer user preferences through single-pass encoding of interaction histories without iterative refinement, relying on increasingly deep architectures to capture complex patterns. In this work, we revisit sequential recommendation from a recursive inference perspective: can user preferences be modeled as a persistent latent state that is recursively refined? We propose RecRec (Recursive Recommendation), a lightweight model that maintains a compact latent state and updates it through a shared recursive module conditioned on interaction evidence. Unlike prior recursive models, RecRec introduces an evidence-anchored correction mechanism that stabilizes refinement by grounding each update in the original interaction context, preventing semantic drift during deep recursive reasoning. Experiments on three benchmark datasets under standard evaluation protocols show that RecRec matches or outperforms state-of-the-art sequential, graph-based, and reasoning-enhanced recommenders while using only 3.9M to 14M parameters. Ablation studies demonstrate that both recursive refinement and the evidence-anchored correction gate contribute significantly to performance, highlighting the effectiveness of recursive latent inference as a scalable alternative to deeper or language-based architectures. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RecRec-6B67/README.md.

</details>


### [298] [Tool-Adaptive LLM Reranker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10555)
*Zichuan Liu,Ruijin Hua*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TALRanker将逐点相关性评分形式化为智能体马尔可夫决策过程，通过两阶段训练（混合损失预热+强化学习优化）使策略在置信时跳过工具提升效率，不确定时检索外部证据避免幻觉，在标准与推理密集检索基准上达到SOTA，匹配逐点重排序器吞吐量且超越参数密集型推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式LLM在复杂查询中易产生事实性幻觉；外部工具调用可缓解但逐文档无差别调用会带来难以承受的延迟开销，形成了精度-效率的根本性困境。

Method: 提出TALRanker框架：1) 将点式相关性评分建模为智能体马尔可夫决策过程；2) 两阶段训练——先用语言保留混合损失预热以防止灾难性遗忘，再用非对称代价感知奖励的强化学习驱动策略自主决策是否调用工具。

Result: 在标准与推理密集型检索基准上达到SOTA性能，吞吐量与逐点重排序器持平，并超越参数密集型推理模型。

Conclusion: TALRanker通过智能体式工具调用策略有效平衡了检索精度与推理效率，为生成式LLM在检索场景中的可信应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generative Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized information retrieval, yet their strictly parametric nature frequently leads to severe factual hallucinations when confronted with complex queries beyond their epistemic boundaries. While external tool-calling can mitigate this, indiscriminately invoking search tools for every document during reranking incurs prohibitive latency overheads, creating an intractable accuracy-efficiency dilemma. To address this challenge, we propose TALRanker, a novel framework that formalizes pointwise relevance scoring as an agentic Markov decision process. We optimize it via a two-stage training paradigm. An initial warm-up utilizes a language-preserving hybrid loss to prevent the catastrophic forgetting of native generative capacities.
